Actually, in membrane proteins, the Pro residues in TM segments don&#x27;t always completely break the helix. They often just create a kink (~20-30° bend) and the helix continues. The segments between Pro breaks in a TM would be too short to be independent helices.

In crystal structures of 12-TM transporters (like LacY, GlpT), there are indeed Pro residues within TM segments that create kinks but don&#x27;t split the segment into two helices. The helix just bends.
